It was a chance encounter. She was happy to see me and we chatted for a while. I asked her if she'd like to grab a coffee and we went to a nearby cafe and continued our conversation. We got on well and the time passed pleasantly. There was a museum quite near the cafe and I asked her if she would like to visit it with me. She agreed and off we went. The museum was quite large and we spent some time there. I was able to act like a typical college teacher and tell her many things about the exhibits. She seemed impressed. We left the museum and made our way back towards the main station, where we would go our seperate ways. On the way to the station we passed a hotel. I asked her if she wanted to go in the hotel and we could talk a little more, but in private away from all the crowds and city noise. She was a little surpised but said yes. So we went into the hotel. Once in the room I switched on the TV and we watched it for a while. It was a chance encounter. She was happy to see me and we chatted for a while. I asked her if she'd like to grab a coffee and we went to a nearby cafe and continued our conversation. We got on well and the time passed pleasantly. There was a museum quite near the cafe and I asked her if she would like to visit it with me. She agreed and off we went. The museum was quite large and we spent some time there. I was able to act like a typical college teacher and tell her many things about the exhibits. She seemed impressed. We left the museum and made our way back towards the main station, where we would go our seperate ways. On the way to the station we passed a hotel. I asked her if she wanted to go in the hotel and we could talk a little more, but in private away from all the crowds and city noise. She was a little surpised but said yes. So we went into the hotel. Once in the room I switched on the TV and we watched it for a while. Then I lay on the bed and asked her if she wanted to lie next to me. She did so but seemed a little tense. As we lay chatting together I turned and placed my hand on her breast over her clothes.</p>"},{"timestamp":"4:12:14 PM","text":"<p>Many years ago I was a college teacher in a foreign country.
